265a4(1): rdo rje rgyu ste 'khor lo che//sangs rgyas 'khor lo gter chen po//dkyil 'khor kun te chos kyi mchog/chos kyi 'khor lo khyod phyag 'tshal//rdo rje gsung ba gsung ba che//spros med yi ge chen po ste//
265a5(1): yi ge med pa bzlas pa che//sangs rgyas gsung khyod phyag 'tshal lo//rdo rje las te las rab mchog/las che las rab byed pa ste//gsang pa'i mchod pa mchod pa ste//sangs rgyas mchod khyod phyag 'tshal lo///
265a6(1): rdo rje bsrung ba go cha che//go cha'i mchog ste brtan pa che//bsrung ba che zhing snying po che//sangs rgyas brtson 'grus phyag 'tshal lo//rdo rje gnod sbyin khro bo che//ma rungs thams cad sreg byed pa//sangs rgyas 
265a7(1): kun gyi thabs chen te//bsung ba mchog khyod phyag 'tshal lo//rdorje bsdams pa phyag rgya che//dam tshig chen po bcings pa che//khu tshur chen po phyag rgya mchog/rdo rje khu tshur khyod phyag 'tshal//de bzhin gshegs 
265b1(1): pas phyag byas shing //brjod cing brjod de bsti stang byas//gang phyir thog mtha ma med //byang chub sems ni khyod yin te //khyod la rten nas rgyal ba dang //dpa' bo byang chub sems dpa' kun//
265b2(1): 'byung zhing yang dag 'byung 'gyur bas //sangs rgyas byang chub mchog gi rgyu //rdo rje sems dpa' khyod phyag 'tshal //rdo rje rin chen khyod phyag 'tshal //rdo rje chos khyod phyag 'tshal lo//rdo rje 
265b3(1): las ni khyod phyag 'tshal //yang dag par ni phyag 'tshal zhing //mtshan mchog gis ni khyod bstod pa'i//bsod nams gang yin de kun yang //sangs rgyas byang chub thob par shog/
265b4(1): mtshan brgya rtsa brgyad dam pa yi//rab dad pas ni lan cig tsam//gang zhig legs par brjod na ni //kun kyang sangs rgyas nyid du 'gyur //zhes sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das kyis gsungs so/
265b5(1): de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po phyag na rdo rje la //de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad gcig tu 'dus te //gsung gcig tu legs so////
265b6(1): zhes bya ba byin no//rdo rje theg pa bla na med //de bzhin gshegs pa kun gyi gsung //theg pa chen po mngon 'dus pa'i //mdo ni legs par bshad pas ni//
266a1(1): @#//rdo rje rin chen khyod legs so//rdo rje chos ni khyod legs so//rdo rje las ni khyod legs so//bcom ldan 'das kyis de skad ces bka' stsal pa dang //
266a2(1): de bzhin gshegs pa dang //'phags pa byang chub sems dpar bcas pa rang gi thugs//la zhugs pa thams cad//dgyes shing mnyes nas//bcom ldan 'das rdo rje sem dpas//
266a3(1): gsungs pa la mngon par da =/par 'gyur to//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi de kho na nyid//bsdus pa zhes bya ba theg//pa chen po'i ma ngo rdzogs s+h+yo////
